Crucially, our analysis reveals an emergent meta-cognitive ability: the model learns to autonomously differentiate between tasks requiring rigorous planning and those favoring direct generation, validating the effectiveness of our direct alignment approach.

</details>


### [191] [Inventory of the 12 007 Low-Dimensional Pseudo-Boolean Landscapes Invariant to Rank, Translation, and Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05530)
*Arnaud Liefooghe,Sébastien Verel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了更强的秩景观不变性概念，通过对布尔函数在维度1、2、3下的不变景观类进行全面分类，揭示了12,007个等价类，并发现非单射函数比单射函数产生更多的不变类，为算法性能分析和基准问题设计提供了基础资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多随机优化算法依赖于解的排序而非绝对适应度值，因此需要更深入理解优化问题间的等价性及其对算法行为的影响。

Method: 引入包含排序、邻域结构和对称性（平移与旋转）的秩景观不变性定义，并对伪布尔函数在低维情况下的所有不变景观类进行穷举分类，涵盖单射与非单射情形。

Result: 共识别出12,007个不变景观类，远少于仅考虑秩不变的情况；发现非单射函数导致更多不变类，且展现出复杂的地形特性（如欺骗性、中性）与算法行为关系。

Conclusion: 该分类结果有助于理解景观难度的本质，支持教学、基准测试设计以及构建具有可控难度的大规模问题，推动优化算法理论发展。

Abstract: Many randomized optimization algorithms are rank-invariant, relying solely on the relative ordering of solutions rather than absolute fitness values. We introduce a stronger notion of rank landscape invariance: two problems are equivalent if their ranking, but also their neighborhood structure and symmetries (translation and rotation), induce identical landscapes. This motivates the study of rank landscapes rather than individual functions. While prior work analyzed the rankings of injective function classes in isolation, we provide an exhaustive inventory of the invariant landscape classes for pseudo-Boolean functions of dimensions 1, 2, and 3, including non-injective cases. Our analysis reveals 12,007 classes in total, a significant reduction compared to rank-invariance alone. We find that non-injective functions yield far more invariant landscape classes than injective ones. In addition, complex combinations of topological landscape properties and algorithm behaviors emerge, particularly regarding deceptiveness, neutrality, and the performance of hill-climbing strategies. The inventory serves as a resource for pedagogical purposes and benchmark design, offering a foundation for constructing larger problems with controlled hardness and advancing our understanding of landscape difficulty and algorithm performance.

</details>


### [192] [A canonical generalization of OBDD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05537)
*Florent Capelli,YooJung Choi,Stefan Mengel,Martín Muñoz,Guy Van den Broeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了树决策图（TDD）作为布尔函数的模型，推广了OBDD，并展示了其在可满足性、计数、枚举等操作上的高效性和更强的简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服OBDD在表示某些CNF公式时的空间局限性，提出更高效的布尔函数表示方法。

Method: 将TDD定义为遵循vtree结构的结构化d-DNNF的特例，并通过自底向上的编译方法分析其复杂性，关联因子宽度概念进行理论分析。

Result: 证明TDD具有与OBDD相同的可追踪性质且更为简洁；展示了树宽为k的CNF公式可用FPT大小的TDD表示，而这对OBDD是不可能的。

Conclusion: TDD是比OBDD更强大的模型，在保持多项式时间操作能力的同时提供了更强的表达能力，适用于更广泛的逻辑公式编译任务。

Abstract: We introduce Tree Decision Diagrams (TDD) as a model for Boolean functions that generalizes OBDD. They can be seen as a restriction of structured d-DNNF; that is, d-DNNF that respect a vtree $T$. We show that TDDs enjoy the same tractability properties as OBDD, such as model counting, enumeration, conditioning, and apply, and are more succinct. In particular, we show that CNF formulas of treewidth $k$ can be represented by TDDs of FPT size, which is known to be impossible for OBDD. We study the complexity of compiling CNF formulas into deterministic TDDs via bottom-up compilation and relate the complexity of this approach with the notion of factor width introduced by Bova and Szeider.

</details>


### [193] [Label Effects: Shared Heuristic Reliance in Trust Assessment by Humans and LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05593)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Sijing Qin,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现，无论是人类还是大语言模型（LLM）在评估信息可信度时，都会受到来源标签的显著影响，倾向于更信任标注为“人类撰写”的内容，即使内容本身相同。通过眼动追踪和内部状态分析，研究揭示了标签作为启发式线索在判断中的主导作用，提示LLM作为评估者可能存在偏倚，对当前依赖人类偏好的对齐方法提出了警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑当前将大语言模型用作自动评估者（LLM-as-a-Judge）的可靠性，探究其是否继承了人类判断中的认知偏倚，特别是对信息来源标签的敏感性。

Method: 采用反事实实验设计，比较相同内容在标注为“人类生成”或“AI生成”时，人类与LLM评估者的信任评分差异；结合眼动追踪技术分析人类注意力分布，并分析LLM在决策过程中的注意力机制与logits不确定性。

Result: 人类和LLM均对标注为“人类撰写”的内容给予更高信任；眼动数据显示人类高度依赖来源标签；LLM在处理时也更关注标签区域，且该效应在‘人类’标签下更强；AI标签条件下的决策不确定性更高。

Conclusion: 来源标签是人类和LLM判断中的显著启发式线索，这挑战了LLM-as-a-Judge的评估效度，并提示当前基于人类偏好的对齐方式可能将人类的认知捷径传递给模型，需发展去偏评估与对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated evaluators (LLM-as-a-Judge). This work challenges its reliability by showing that trust judgments by LLMs are biased by disclosed source labels. Using a counterfactual design, we find that both humans and LLM judges assign higher trust to information labeled as human-authored than to the same content labeled as AI-generated. Eye-tracking data reveal that humans rely heavily on source labels as heuristic cues for judgments. We analyze LLM internal states during judgment. Across label conditions, models allocate denser attention to the label region than the content region, and this label dominance is stronger under Human labels than AI labels, consistent with the human gaze patterns. Besides, decision uncertainty measured by logits is higher under AI labels than Human labels. These results indicate that the source label is a salient heuristic cue for both humans and LLMs. It raises validity concerns for label-sensitive LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation, and we cautiously raise that aligning models with human preferences may propagate human heuristic reliance into models, motivating debiased evaluation and alignment.

</details>


### [194] [Beyond Behavior: Why AI Evaluation Needs a Cognitive Revolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05631)
*Amir Konigsberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为图灵测试所体现的行为主义认识论长期限制了人工智能研究，使其无法探讨与智能归因相关的内部机制问题，主张AI领域需要一场类似心理学‘认知革命’的认识论转变，超越单纯依赖行为证据的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图揭示图灵提出的行为主义标准对AI研究的深层影响，指出当前仅依赖外在表现评估智能的做法阻碍了对系统内部过程和机制的追问，限制了对‘智能’本身的科学理解。

Method: 通过分析图灵测试的认识论基础，并类比心理学从行为主义到认知主义的范式转变，本文进行哲学与历史的批判性梳理，论证AI评估体系中行为主义取向的局限性。

Result: 揭示了AI领域长期以来受行为主义认识论支配，导致无法有效区分实现相同输出但机制迥异的系统，从而难以做出合理的智能归因；并指出现有评估框架使某些关键科学问题变得‘不可问’。

Conclusion: AI需要一次认识论上的‘认知革命’，在保留行为证据的同时，承认其不足，发展出能够探究系统内部结构与过程的新评估范式，以支持更严谨的智能主张。

Abstract: In 1950, Alan Turing proposed replacing the question "Can machines think?" with a behavioral test: if a machine's outputs are indistinguishable from those of a thinking being, the question of whether it truly thinks can be set aside. This paper argues that Turing's move was not only a pragmatic simplification but also an epistemological commitment, a decision about what kind of evidence counts as relevant to intelligence attribution, and that this commitment has quietly constrained AI research for seven decades. We trace how Turing's behavioral epistemology became embedded in the field's evaluative infrastructure, rendering unaskable a class of questions about process, mechanism, and internal organization that cognitive psychology, neuroscience, and related disciplines learned to ask. We draw a structural parallel to the behaviorist-to-cognitivist transition in psychology: just as psychology's commitment to studying only observable behavior prevented it from asking productive questions about internal mental processes until that commitment was abandoned, AI's commitment to behavioral evaluation prevents it from distinguishing between systems that achieve identical outputs through fundamentally different computational processes, a distinction on which intelligence attribution depends. We argue that the field requires an epistemological transition comparable to the cognitive revolution: not an abandonment of behavioral evidence, but a recognition that behavioral evidence alone is insufficient for the construct claims the field wishes to make. We articulate what a post-behaviorist epistemology for AI would involve and identify the specific questions it would make askable that the field currently has no way to ask.

</details>


### [195] [PECKER: A Precisely Efficient Critical Knowledge Erasure Recipe For Machine Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05634)
*Zhiyong Ma,Zhitao Deng,Huan Tang,Jialin Chen,Zhijun Zheng,Zhengping Li,Qingyuan Chuai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PECKER是一种高效的机器遗忘方法，通过引入显著性掩码优化参数更新，减少不必要的梯度计算，在保证遗忘效果的同时显著缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法存在训练时间和计算开销过高的问题，主要源于梯度更新方向不佳，影响训练效率和收敛稳定性。

Method: 在知识蒸馏框架下，PECKER引入显著性掩码，优先更新对目标数据遗忘贡献最大的参数，从而减少冗余梯度计算。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上，PECKER在类别遗忘和概念遗忘任务中均实现了更快的训练速度，同时保持与真实图像分布的高度一致。

Conclusion: PECKER在不牺牲遗忘效果的前提下，显著提升了机器遗忘过程的训练效率和稳定性，优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning (MU) has become a critical technique for GenAI models' safe and compliant operation. While existing MU methods are effective, most impose prohibitive training time and computational overhead. Our analysis suggests the root cause lies in poorly directed gradient updates, which reduce training efficiency and destabilize convergence. To mitigate these issues, we propose PECKER, an efficient MU approach that matches or outperforms prevailing methods. Within a distillation framework, PECKER introduces a saliency mask to prioritize updates to parameters that contribute most to forgetting the targeted data, thereby reducing unnecessary gradient computation and shortening overall training time without sacrificing unlearning efficacy. Our method generates samples that unlearn related class or concept more quickly, while closely aligning with the true image distribution on CIFAR-10 and STL-10 datasets, achieving shorter training times for both class forgetting and concept forgetting.

</details>


### [196] [QA-MoE: Towards a Continuous Reliability Spectrum with Quality-Aware Mixture of Experts for Robust Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05704)
*Yitong Zhu,Yuxuan Jiang,Guanxuan Jiang,Bojing Hou,Peng Yuan Zhou,Ge Lin Kan,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态情感分析框架QA-MoE，通过连续可靠性谱和自监督的不确定性估计来处理动态噪声和模态缺失问题，实现了鲁棒且通用的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以应对实际场景中多模态输入的动态噪声和模态缺失问题，且通常假设固定的损坏比例，缺乏对连续变化的可靠性条件的适应能力。

Method: 引入连续可靠性谱统一建模模态缺失与质量退化，并提出QA-MoE框架，利用自监督的aleatoric不确定性量化模态可靠性，指导专家路由机制，抑制不可靠信号的误差传播并保留任务相关的信息。

Result: 实验表明QA-MoE在多种退化场景下达到竞争性或最先进的性能，并展现出‘一个检查点适用于所有情况’的实用特性。

Conclusion: QA-MoE能够有效应对多模态输入中动态变化的可靠性问题，为现实场景下的多模态情感分析提供了更鲁棒和灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) aims to infer human sentiment from textual, acoustic, and visual signals. In real-world scenarios, however, multimodal inputs are often compromised by dynamic noise or modality missingness. Existing methods typically treat these imperfections as discrete cases or assume fixed corruption ratios, which limits their adaptability to continuously varying reliability conditions. To address this, we first introduce a Continuous Reliability Spectrum to unify missingness and quality degradation into a single framework. Building on this, we propose QA-MoE, a Quality-Aware Mixture-of-Experts framework that quantifies modality reliability via self-supervised aleatoric uncertainty. This mechanism explicitly guides expert routing, enabling the model to suppress error propagation from unreliable signals while preserving task-relevant information. Extensive experiments indicate that QA-MoE achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance across diverse degradation scenarios and exhibits a promising One-Checkpoint-for-All property in practice.

</details>


### [197] [Can Large Language Models Reinvent Foundational Algorithms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05716)
*Jian Zhao,Haoren Luo,Yu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Pingyue Sheng,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出“遗忘与重发明”框架，探索大语言模型是否能重新发明计算机科学中的基础算法，结果表明在适当提示和强化学习支持下，部分模型具备一定的重发明能力，但复杂算法仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否具备基础创新能力，关键在于其能否重新发明已有基础算法，这是实现科学原创发现的前提。

Method: 采用基于GRPO的策略梯度方法进行模型知识遗忘，并设计控制环境测试模型在不同提示等级下重新发明10种经典算法的能力，结合生成式验证器与测试时强化学习提升推理稳定性。

Result: Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507模型在无提示、一级提示和二级提示下分别成功重发明50%、70%和90%的算法；少数高层提示可显著提升成功率，但逐步提示仍无法解决复杂算法的重发明问题；测试时强化学习使Strassen算法在二级提示下成功被重发明；生成式验证器有助于维持模型推理能力，避免‘思维崩溃’。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在外部引导和机制支持下展现出一定程度的基础算法重发明能力，表明其具备初步的创新潜力，但仍受限于复杂问题的自主推理能力，距离真正的基础创新仍有差距。

Abstract: LLMs have shown strong potential to advance scientific discovery. Whether they possess the capacity for foundational innovation, however, remains an open question. In this work, we focus on a prerequisite for foundational innovation: can LLMs reinvent foundational algorithms in computer science? Our \textit{Unlearn-and-Reinvent} pipeline applies LLM unlearning to remove a specific foundational algorithm, such as Dijkstra's or Euclid's algorithm, from an LLM's pretrained knowledge, and then tests whether the model can reinvent it in a controlled environment. To enable effective unlearning, we adopt a GRPO-based, on-policy unlearning method. Across 10 target algorithms, 3 strong open-weight models, and 3 hint levels, our experiments demonstrate that (1) the strongest model Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 successfully reinvents 50% of the algorithms with no hint, 70% at hint level 1, and 90% at hint level 2; (2) a few high-level hints can enhance the reinvention success rate, but even step-by-step hints fail for those complicated algorithms; and (3) test-time reinforcement learning enables successful reinvention for the Strassen algorithm at hint level 2. Through analyses of output trajectories and ablation studies, we find that generative verifier in the reinvention phase plays a critical role in sustaining models' reasoning strength, helping to avoid the ``thought collapse'' phenomenon. These findings offer insights into both the potential and current limits of LLMs' innovative thinking.

</details>


### [198] [Emergent social transmission of model-based representations without inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05777)
*Silja Keßler,Miriam Bautista-Salinero,Claudio Tennie,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，即使不依赖复杂的心理推断，人类也能通过简单的社会线索和非心智化过程实现文化传递，利用强化学习模拟发现观察性学习可促进知识的传播与表征趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人类如何在有限认知能力下，从他人处获得丰富的环境知识，挑战了传统上认为需要高成本心智化推理的观点。

Method: 使用强化学习模拟，构建一个仅通过观察专家行为（而不推断其心理状态）进行学习的 naive agent，比较单独学习与观察学习的效果。

Result: 模拟显示，基于简单社会线索的学习策略能有效引导学习者经验偏差，使其表征逐渐接近专家；尤其是基于模型的学习者表现出更快的学习速度和更相似的内部表征。

Conclusion: 文化传递可以通过简单、非心智化的社会学习机制实现，无需推断他人信念，而是利用现有的非社会学习机制即可达成高效的知识传承。

Abstract: How do people acquire rich, flexible knowledge about their environment from others despite limited cognitive capacity? Humans are often thought to rely on computationally costly mentalizing, such as inferring others' beliefs. In contrast, cultural evolution emphasizes that behavioral transmission can be supported by simple social cues. Using reinforcement learning simulations, we show how minimal social learning can indirectly transmit higher-level representations. We simulate a naïve agent searching for rewards in a reconfigurable environment, learning either alone or by observing an expert - crucially, without inferring mental states. Instead, the learner heuristically selects actions or boosts value representations based on observed actions. Our results demonstrate that these cues bias the learner's experience, causing its representation to converge toward the expert's. Model-based learners benefit most from social exposure, showing faster learning and more expert-like representations. These findings show how cultural transmission can arise from simple, non-mentalizing processes exploiting asocial learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [199] [Vision-Guided Iterative Refinement for Frontend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05839)
*Hannah Sansford,Derek H. C. Law,Wei Liu,Abhishek Tripathi,Niresh Agarwal,Gerrit J. J. van den Burg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化批评循环框架，用于指导前端代码生成的迭代优化，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多阶段人工反馈机制在前端开发中成本高昂，尤其依赖可视化输出的质量评估，需要更高效的自动化方法。

Method: 构建一个以视觉语言模型作为视觉批评者的自动化批评循环框架，并结合LoRA进行参数高效微调，探索批评信息能否被代码生成模型内化。

Result: 在WebDev Arena数据集上，经过三轮迭代优化性能最高提升17.8%；微调后可实现最佳批评循环效果的25%，且未显著增加token消耗。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的自动化批评能显著提升前端代码生成质量，优于单次生成，凸显了迭代优化对复杂视觉输出的重要性。

Abstract: Code generation with large language models often relies on multi-stage human-in-the-loop refinement, which is effective but very costly - particularly in domains such as frontend web development where the solution quality depends on rendered visual output. We present a fully automated critic-in-the-loop framework in which a vision-language model serves as a visual critic that provides structured feedback on rendered webpages to guide iterative refinement of generated code. Across real-world user requests from the WebDev Arena dataset, this approach yields consistent improvements in solution quality, achieving up to 17.8% increase in performance over three refinement cycles. Next, we investigate parameter-efficient fine-tuning using LoRA to understand whether the improvements provided by the critic can be internalized by the code-generating LLM. Fine-tuning achieves 25% of the gains from the best critic-in-the-loop solution without a significant increase in token counts. Our findings indicate that automated, VLM-based critique of frontend code generation leads to significantly higher quality solutions than can be achieved through a single LLM inference pass, and highlight the importance of iterative refinement for the complex visual outputs associated with web development.

</details>


### [200] [When Do We Need LLMs? A Diagnostic for Language-Driven Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05859)
*Uljad Berdica,Fernando Acero,Anton Ipsen,Parisa Zehtabi,Michael Cashmore,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LLMP-UCB的上下文多臂赌博机算法，利用大语言模型（LLM）生成不确定性估计，但发现基于文本嵌入的轻量级数值赌博机在成本远低于LLM的情况下性能相当甚至更优。研究还表明嵌入维度可调节探索与利用的平衡，并提出了一个基于臂嵌入几何特征的诊断方法，指导何时使用LLM推理或轻量级方法，为金融等领域的AI决策系统提供了高效、低成本的部署框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含文本和数值信息的非 episodic 序列决策问题中，虽然大语言模型（LLMs）被广泛应用，但每步决策都使用LLM进行推理计算成本高且难以获得可靠的不确定性估计。因此需要一种更高效、低成本并能提供不确定性感知的决策方法。

Method: 提出LLMP-UCB算法，通过重复推理从LLM中提取不确定性估计；同时比较使用密集或Matryoshka文本嵌入的轻量级数值赌博机的表现；引入基于臂嵌入几何结构的诊断工具，以决定何时使用LLM或轻量级方法。

Result: 实验表明，基于文本嵌入的轻量级数值赌博机在准确率上达到甚至超过LLM方法，且成本显著更低；嵌入维度可有效调节探索-利用权衡；提出的几何诊断有助于实践者选择合适的方法。

Conclusion: 在文本增强的上下文多臂赌博机问题中，轻量级数值方法结合嵌入表示可媲美LLM性能，且更具成本效益；LLMP-UCB提供了不确定性估计的新路径，而嵌入维度和几何诊断为实际部署提供了灵活、原则性的优化手段。

Abstract: We study Contextual Multi-Armed Bandits (CMABs) for non-episodic sequential decision making problems where the context includes both textual and numerical information (e.g., recommendation systems, dynamic portfolio adjustments, offer selection; all frequent problems in finance). While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to these settings, utilizing LLMs for reasoning at every decision step is computationally expensive and uncertainty estimates are difficult to obtain. To address this, we introduce LLMP-UCB, a bandit algorithm that derives uncertainty estimates from LLMs via repeated inference. However, our experiments demonstrate that lightweight numerical bandits operating on text embeddings (dense or Matryoshka) match or exceed the accuracy of LLM-based solutions at a fraction of their cost. We further show that embedding dimensionality is a practical lever on the exploration-exploitation balance, enabling cost--performance tradeoffs without prompt complexity. Finally, to guide practitioners, we propose a geometric diagnostic based on the arms' embedding to decide when to use LLM-driven reasoning versus a lightweight numerical bandit. Our results provide a principled deployment framework for cost-effective, uncertainty-aware decision systems with broad applicability across AI use cases in financial services.

</details>


### [201] [HybridKV: Hybrid KV Cache Compression for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05887)
*Bowen Zeng,Feiyang Ren,Jun Zhang,Xiaoling Gu,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HybridKV的混合键值缓存压缩框架，通过分类注意力头并应用不同压缩策略，在大幅减少多模态大语言模型推理内存开销的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理视觉输入时会产生巨大的键值缓存，导致内存消耗和延迟问题，现有压缩方法忽略了不同注意力头的行为差异，缺乏针对性的压缩策略。

Method: 首先基于文本中心注意力将注意力头分为静态和动态类型；然后采用自上而下的层级方式分配缓存预算；最后对静态头使用基于文本先验的剪枝，对动态头使用分块检索进行压缩。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上于11个多模态基准测试中验证，HybridKV最多可将键值缓存内存减少7.9倍，并实现1.52倍更快的解码速度，且几乎无性能损失甚至有所提升。

Conclusion: HybridKV通过结合多种互补压缩策略，有效解决了MLLMs推理过程中因视觉输入引起的高内存占用和延迟问题，为高效多模态推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced unified reasoning over text, images, and videos, but their inference is hindered by the rapid growth of key-value (KV) caches. Each visual input expands into thousands of tokens, causing caches to scale linearly with context length and remain resident in GPU memory throughout decoding, which leads to prohibitive memory overhead and latency even on high-end GPUs. A common solution is to compress caches under a fixed allocated budget at different granularities: token-level uniformly discards less important tokens, layer-level varies retention across layers, and head-level redistributes budgets across heads. Yet these approaches stop at allocation and overlook the heterogeneous behaviors of attention heads that require distinct compression strategies. We propose HybridKV, a hybrid KV cache compression framework that integrates complementary strategies in three stages: heads are first classified into static or dynamic types using text-centric attention; then a top-down budget allocation scheme hierarchically assigns KV budgets; finally, static heads are compressed by text-prior pruning and dynamic heads by chunk-wise retrieval. Experiments on 11 multimodal benchmarks with Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that HybridKV reduces KV cache memory by up to $7.9\times$ and achieves $1.52\times$ faster decoding, with almost no performance drop or even higher relative to the full-cache MLLM.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Compromise: Pareto-Lenient Consensus for Efficient Multi-Preference LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05965)
*Renxuan Tan,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Pareto-Lenient Consensus (PLC) 的博弈论框架，用于解决多目标偏好对齐中的局部停滞问题，通过动态容忍短期性能下降以实现全局帕累托优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标偏好对齐方法通常采用静态线性标量化或刚性梯度投影，容易陷入局部最优，牺牲了潜在的全局帕累托改进。

Method: 提出PLC框架，将对齐视为动态协商过程，引入基于共识的宽容梯度修正机制，在存在足够主导联盟盈余时允许局部性能退化，从而帮助优化路径逃离局部次优均衡。

Result: 理论分析表明PLC可实现僵局突破并渐近收敛至帕累托共识均衡；实验结果显示其在固定偏好对齐和全局帕累托前沿质量上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PLC为多目标偏好对齐提供了新思路，展示了基于协商的对齐方法在提升模型对齐质量方面的潜力。

Abstract: Transcending the single-preference paradigm, aligning LLMs with diverse human values is pivotal for robust deployment. Contemporary Multi-Objective Preference Alignment (MPA) approaches predominantly rely on static linear scalarization or rigid gradient projection to navigate these trade-offs. However, by enforcing strict conflict avoidance or simultaneous descent, these paradigms often prematurely converge to local stationary points. While mathematically stable, these points represent a conservative compromise where the model sacrifices potential global Pareto improvements to avoid transient local trade-offs. To break this deadlock, we propose Pareto-Lenient Consensus (PLC), a game-theoretic framework that reimagines alignment as a dynamic negotiation process. Unlike rigid approaches, PLC introduces consensus-driven lenient gradient rectification, which dynamically tolerates local degradation provided there is a sufficient dominant coalition surplus, thereby empowering the optimization trajectory to escape local suboptimal equilibrium and explore the distal Pareto-optimal frontier. Theoretical analysis validates PLC can facilitate stalemate escape and asymptotically converge to a Pareto consensus equilibrium. Moreover, extensive experiments show that PLC surpasses baselines in both fixed-preference alignment and global Pareto frontier quality. This work highlights the potential of negotiation-driven alignment as a promising avenue for MPA. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/aaa-6BB8.

</details>


### [203] [How LLMs Follow Instructions: Skillful Coordination, Not a Universal Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06015)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令调优并非依赖单一通用机制，而是通过协调多种语言能力来实现指令跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究指令调优模型中指令跟随能力的本质机制，判断其是基于通用机制还是组合技能的运用。

Method: 通过对三个指令调优模型在九个不同任务上的诊断探针分析，评估跨任务表现、表征共享程度、技能迁移模式及因果依赖关系。

Result: 发现通用探针性能低于任务专用探针；跨任务迁移弱且按技能相似性聚类；因果消融显示稀疏非对称依赖；任务复杂度在层级上分层，结构约束早现，语义任务晚现；时间分析表明约束满足是生成过程中的动态监控而非预先规划。

Conclusion: 指令跟随更可能是多种语言技能的协调运作，而非单一抽象约束检查机制的激活。

Abstract: Instruction tuning is commonly assumed to endow language models with a domain-general ability to follow instructions, yet the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. Does instruction-following rely on a universal mechanism or compositional skill deployment? We investigate this through diagnostic probing across nine diverse tasks in three instruction-tuned models.
  Our analysis provides converging evidence against a universal mechanism. First, general probes trained across all tasks consistently underperform task-specific specialists, indicating limited representational sharing. Second, cross-task transfer is weak and clustered by skill similarity. Third, causal ablation reveals sparse asymmetric dependencies rather than shared representations. Tasks also stratify by complexity across layers, with structural constraints emerging early and semantic tasks emerging late. Finally, temporal analysis shows constraint satisfaction operates as dynamic monitoring during generation rather than pre-generation planning.
  These findings indicate that instruction-following is better characterized as skillful coordination of diverse linguistic capabilities rather than deployment of a single abstract constraint-checking process.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [From Video to Control: A Survey of Learning Manipulation Interfaces from Temporal Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04974)
*Linfang Zheng,Zikai Ouyang,Chen Wang,Jia Pan,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文综述了利用无动作标注的视频数据学习机器人操控接口的方法，提出了以接口为中心的分类体系，并分析了三类主要方法在控制集成方面的特性，指出当前最大的挑战在于如何将视频预测有效整合到可靠的机器人行为中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频包含丰富的物理动态信息，但缺乏动作监督且与机器人实际经验存在差异，如何将其转化为可靠的机器人控制仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出了一种以接口为中心的分类法，将方法分为三类：直接视频-动作策略、潜在动作方法和显式视觉接口，并分析每类方法在控制回路闭合、执行前可验证性及故障来源方面的特性。

Result: 识别出三类主流方法及其控制集成特性，发现当前最紧迫的挑战集中在‘机器人集成层’，即连接视频预测与可靠机器人行为的机制。

Conclusion: 未来的研究应聚焦于加强视频预测与机器人控制之间的集成层，提升系统可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Video is a scalable observation of physical dynamics: it captures how objects move, how contact unfolds, and how scenes evolve under interaction -- all without requiring robot action labels. Yet translating this temporal structure into reliable robotic control remains an open challenge, because video lacks action supervision and differs from robot experience in embodiment, viewpoint, and physical constraints. This survey reviews methods that exploit non-action-annotated temporal video to learn control interfaces for robotic manipulation. We introduce an \emph{interface-centric taxonomy} organized by where the video-to-control interface is constructed and what control properties it enables, identifying three families: direct video--action policies, which keep the interface implicit; latent-action methods, which route temporal structure through a compact learned intermediate; and explicit visual interfaces, which predict interpretable targets for downstream control. For each family, we analyze control-integration properties -- how the loop is closed, what can be verified before execution, and where failures enter. A cross-family synthesis reveals that the most pressing open challenges center on the \emph{robotics integration layer} -- the mechanisms that connect video-derived predictions to dependable robot behavior -- and we outline research directions toward closing this gap.

</details>


### [205] [COMB: Common Open Modular robotic platform for Bees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04980)
*Pranav Kedia,Marie Messerich,Tim Landgraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为COMB的紧凑型、开源、模块化机电平台，用于在标准观察蜂箱框架内进行蜂巢内部实验，具备精确控制和多种功能模块支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在真实蜂群中进行可重复且可控的实验，需要一种能够适应空间限制、耐受环境影响并支持传感与局部驱动的机器人系统。

Method: 设计并实现了一个集成XY定位平台、可移动访问窗口（MAW）、可更换载荷模块和嵌入式控制架构的模块化平台COMB，并通过三种典型模块验证其功能。

Result: COMB平台成功实现了轨迹跟踪、组合图像拼接和基于视频的频谱分析，展示了其在蜂巢内稳定执行感知与驱动任务的能力。

Conclusion: COMB是一种可重用、通用化的蜂巢内实验机器人平台，为蜜蜂行为研究提供了灵活且可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: Experimental access to real honeybee colonies requires robotic systems capable of operating within limited spatial constraints, tolerating hive-specific fouling and environmental conditions, and supporting both sensing and localized actuation without frequent hardware redesign. This paper introduces COMB, a compact, open-source, modular mechatronic platform designed for in-hive experiments within standard observation-hive frames. The platform integrates a XY positioning stage, a Movable Access Window (MAW) for sealed tool access through the hive boundary, interchangeable payload modules, and an embedded control architecture that enables repeatable trajectory execution and signal generation. The platform's capabilities are demonstrated through three representative modules: a biomimetic dance-and-signaling payload, a close-range comb scanner, and an electromagnetic wing actuator for localized oscillatory stimulation. This paper details the hardware and software design of COMB, outlines its operational capabilities, and describes the supporting infrastructure for conducting real-world in-hive experiments. The platform is characterized in engineering terms through tracking waggle-trajectory executions, performing multi-image stitching for repeated comb mosaics, and conducting video-based spectral analysis of the wing actuator. These results position COMB as a reusable experimental robotics platform for controlled in-hive sensing and actuation, and as a compact, generalized successor to earlier task-specific honeybee robotic systems.

</details>


### [206] [A Survey on Sensor-based Planning and Control for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05003)
*Shivam Vishwakarma,Tejal Bedmutha,Dharmendra Kumar Patel,Vijay Bhaskar Semwal,Leena Vachhani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于传感器的无人水下航行器（UUV）规划与控制方法，重点分析了反应式局部规划层在复杂水下环境中的应用，并对解耦与耦合架构进行了分类和比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于水下环境复杂且存在GNSS信号缺失、传感器噪声和通信延迟等问题，UUV需要更先进的感知-规划-控制一体化方法以提升自主导航能力。

Method: 对现有基于传感器的规划与控制方法进行系统性综述，将其分为解耦和耦合两类架构，并比较不同控制器（如PID、MPC和不变集控制器）在动态环境中的性能。

Result: 揭示了耦合架构具有更强的实时响应能力；MPC在路径优化方面表现优异但计算开销大；不变集控制器能提供安全性保障但可能牺牲灵活性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种整合规划与控制的架构分类法，强调自适应局部规划的重要性，为UUV在动态海洋环境中的自主导航提供了理论支持和技术分析框架。

Abstract: This survey examines recent sensor-based planning and control methods for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs). In complex, uncertain underwater environments, UUVs require advanced planning and control strategies for effective navigation. These vehicles face significant challenges including drifting and noisy sensor measurements, absence of Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals, and low-bandwidth, high-latency underwater acoustic communications. The focus is on reactive local planning layers that adapt to real-time sensor inputs such as SONAR and Inertial Measurement Units (IMU) to improve localization accuracy and autonomy in dynamic ocean conditions, enabling dynamic obstacle avoidance and on-the-fly re-planning. The survey categorizes the existing literature into decoupled and coupled architectures for sensor-based planning and control. The decoupled architecture sequentially addresses planning and control stages, whereas coupled architectures offer tighter feedback loops for more immediate responsiveness. A comparative analysis of coupled planning and control methods reveals that while PID controllers are simple, they lack predictive capability for complex maneuvers. Model Predictive Control (MPC) offers superior path optimization but can be computationally intensive, and invariant-set controllers provide strong safety guarantees at the potential cost of agility in confined environments. Key contributions include a taxonomy of architectures combining planning and control, a focus on adaptive local planning, and an analysis of controller roles in integrated planning frameworks for autonomous navigation of UUVs.

</details>


### [207] [StarVLA: A Lego-like Codebase for Vision-Language-Action Model Developing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05014)
*StarVLA Community*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StarVLA是一个开源的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）研究框架，提供模块化架构、统一评估接口和可复用训练策略，支持多种骨干模型和基准测试，旨在促进VLA方法的可比性、可复现性和快速原型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA方法在架构、代码库和评估协议上碎片化，导致难以进行公平比较和复现，限制了领域发展。

Method: 提出StarVLA框架，采用模块化的骨干-动作头架构，支持VLM和世界模型骨干及多种动作解码范式；设计跨具身学习和多模态联合训练等通用训练策略；集成多个主流基准测试并提供统一评估接口，支持仿真与真实机器人部署。

Result: 框架实现了对Qwen-VL、Cosmos等骨干模型的支持，在LIBERO、SimplerEnv等多个基准上通过简洁的训练流程达到或超过先前方法性能，并提供完全可复现的训练示例。

Conclusion: StarVLA是目前最全面的开源VLA框架之一，显著降低了复现现有方法和开发新方法的门槛，有望推动VLA领域的标准化和快速发展。

Abstract: Building generalist embodied agents requires integrating perception, language understanding, and action, which are core capabilities addressed by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) approaches based on multimodal foundation models, including recent advances in vision-language models and world models. Despite rapid progress, VLA methods remain fragmented across incompatible architectures, codebases, and evaluation protocols, hindering principled comparison and reproducibility. We present StarVLA, an open-source codebase for VLA research. StarVLA addresses these challenges in three aspects. First, it provides a modular backbone--action-head architecture that supports both VLM backbones (e.g., Qwen-VL) and world-model backbones (e.g., Cosmos) alongside representative action-decoding paradigms, all under a shared abstraction in which backbone and action head can each be swapped independently. Second, it provides reusable training strategies, including cross-embodiment learning and multimodal co-training, that apply consistently across supported paradigms. Third, it integrates major benchmarks, including LIBERO, SimplerEnv, RoboTwin~2.0, RoboCasa-GR1, and BEHAVIOR-1K, through a unified evaluation interface that supports both simulation and real-robot deployment. StarVLA also ships simple, fully reproducible single-benchmark training recipes that, despite minimal data engineering, already match or surpass prior methods on multiple benchmarks with both VLM and world-model backbones. To our best knowledge, StarVLA is one of the most comprehensive open-source VLA frameworks available, and we expect it to lower the barrier for reproducing existing methods and prototyping new ones. StarVLA is being actively maintained and expanded; we will update this report as the project evolves. The code and documentation are available at https://github.com/starVLA/starVLA.

</details>


### [208] [Bilinear Model Predictive Control Framework of the OncoReach, a Tendon-Driven Steerable Stylet for Brachytherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05111)
*Pejman Kheradmand,Behnam Moradkhani,Mir Masoud Ale Ali,Keith Sowards,Scott R. Silva,Yash Chitalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于临床可用的经组织间插植近距离治疗针的肌腱驱动可转向风格的双线性模型预测控制框架，通过仿真和物理实验验证了其在固定目标定位和移动目标轨迹跟踪中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的建模与控制方法主要针对定制化针具设计，难以直接应用于 commercially available 临床针具，因此需要开发一种兼容现有临床设备的可转向风格控制系统。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个虚拟输入（插入速度和两个弯曲速率）的几何双线性模型，并将其映射到实际可实现的输入（插入速度和相应的肌腱张力），采用模型预测控制策略进行控制设计。

Result: 开环模型验证中估计误差低于2 mm（占插入长度的3%），闭环固定目标跟踪误差低至1.45 mm（1.7%），但在某些弯曲方向上实验误差较大，达到8.3 mm（7.8%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明所提出的控制框架在临床兼容的可转向近距离治疗系统中具有应用潜力，但未来需在标定和感知方面进一步改进以提升精度。

Abstract: Steerable needles have the potential to improve interstitial brachytherapy by enabling curved trajectories that avoid sensitive anatomical structures. However, existing modeling and control approaches are primarily developed for custom needle designs and are not directly applicable to stylets compatible with commercially available clinical needles. This paper presents a bilinear model predictive control (MPC) framework for a tendon-driven steerable stylet integrated with a standard brachytherapy needle. \textcolor{black}{A geometric bilinear model is formulated with three virtual inputs (an insertion speed and two bending rates) which are mapped to physically realizable inputs consisting of the insertion speed and the associated tendon tensions.} The approach is validated through simulations and physical insertion experiments in tissue-mimicking phantom material using image-based tip tracking. While open-loop model validation yielded estimation errors below $2$~mm, corresponding to $3\%$ of the inserted needle length, and closed-loop fixed-target tracking achieved an error as low as $1.45$~mm, corresponding to $1.7\%$ of the inserted length, experiments showed larger position errors in certain bending directions, reaching $8.3$~mm, or $7.8\%$ of the inserted length. Overall, the results demonstrate the feasibility of fixed-target positioning and moving-target trajectory tracking for clinically compatible steerable brachytherapy systems, while highlighting necessary areas for future improvements in calibration and sensing.

</details>


### [209] [RoboPlayground: Democratizing Robotic Evaluation through Structured Physical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05226)
*Yi Ru Wang,Carter Ung,Evan Gubarev,Christopher Tan,Siddhartha Srinivasa,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RoboPlayground的框架，通过自然语言驱动的方式实现对机器人操作策略的灵活评估，支持用户以自然语言定义可执行的任务，并揭示了传统固定基准测试难以发现的泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作评估依赖于少数专家设计的固定基准，限制了任务的扩展性和多样性，难以反映真实场景中任务意图和成功标准的变化，因此需要一种更灵活、开放的评估范式。

Method: 提出RoboPlayground框架，将自然语言指令编译为包含资产定义、初始化分布和成功谓词的可执行任务规范，在结构化物理域（如积木操作）中实现语言驱动的任务生成与评估。

Result: 用户研究表明该语言接口比基于编程的方法更易用且认知负担更低；在语言定义的任务族上评估学习策略时发现了固定基准无法显现的泛化失败；任务多样性随贡献者多样性增加而提升，支持通过众包持续扩展评估空间。

Conclusion: 将机器人操作评估重构为语言驱动的过程，能够提高评估的可访问性、可控性和可扩展性，RoboPlayground为未来开放式、多样化评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Evaluation of robotic manipulation systems has largely relied on fixed benchmarks authored by a small number of experts, where task instances, constraints, and success criteria are predefined and difficult to extend. This paradigm limits who can shape evaluation and obscures how policies respond to user-authored variations in task intent, constraints, and notions of success. We argue that evaluating modern manipulation policies requires reframing evaluation as a language-driven process over structured physical domains. We present RoboPlayground, a framework that enables users to author executable manipulation tasks using natural language within a structured physical domain. Natural language instructions are compiled into reproducible task specifications with explicit asset definitions, initialization distributions, and success predicates. Each instruction defines a structured family of related tasks, enabling controlled semantic and behavioral variation while preserving executability and comparability. We instantiate RoboPlayground in a structured block manipulation domain and evaluate it along three axes. A user study shows that the language-driven interface is easier to use and imposes lower cognitive workload than programming-based and code-assist baselines. Evaluating learned policies on language-defined task families reveals generalization failures that are not apparent under fixed benchmark evaluations. Finally, we show that task diversity scales with contributor diversity rather than task count alone, enabling evaluation spaces to grow continuously through crowd-authored contributions. Project Page: https://roboplayground.github.io

</details>


### [210] [ZipFold: Modular Actuators for Scaleable Adaptive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05260)
*Niklas Hagemann,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑且易于制造的可展开执行器，通过复合折叠和拉链式结构将柔性3D打印塑料条转化为方形截面梁，实现可逆的尺度与刚度变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对当前形状变化系统多依赖定制化机制、难以扩展和跨应用迁移的问题，需要一种通用、可重构的解决方案。

Method: 利用柔性3D打印塑料带的复合折叠和拉链式展开机制，构建可逆转变的模块化执行器，并将其集成到多模块系统中实现形态与刚度调控。

Result: 实现了执行器在紧凑（柔性）与展开（准刚性）状态间的平滑连续转换，展示了四模块自适应行走机器人的集成应用。

Conclusion: 该执行器具有良好的可扩展性和可重构性，为适应性机器人系统提供了一种通用且高效的实现路径。

Abstract: There is a growing need for robots that can change their shape, size and mechanical properties to adapt to evolving tasks and environments. However, current shape-changing systems generally utilize bespoke, system-specific mechanisms that can be difficult to scale, reconfigure or translate from one application to another. This paper introduces a compact, easy-to-fabricate deployable actuator that achieves reversible scale and stiffness transformations through compound folding and zipping of flexible 3D-printed plastic strips into square-section deployable beams. The simple actuation method allows for smooth, continuous transitions between compact (flexible) and expanded (quasi-rigid) states, facilitating diverse shape and stiffness transformations when modules are combined into larger assemblies. The actuator's mechanical performance is characterized and an integrated system involving a four-module adaptive walking robot is demonstrated.

</details>


### [211] [Final Report, Center for Computer-Integrated Computer-Integrated Surgical Systems and Technology, NSF ERC Cooperative Agreement EEC9731748, Volume 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05272)
*Russell H. Taylor,Gregory D. Hager,Ralph Etienne-Cummings. Eric Grimson,Ron Kikinis,Cameron Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 过去十年，医疗机器人从边缘走向主流，在CISST ERC和美国国家科学基金会支持下，推动了手术系统的智能化发展，显著提升了医疗精度、安全性和效率，并降低成本，造福患者与整个医疗体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动医疗机器人技术的发展，将数据与技术融合到临床系统中，以革新手术及其他医疗操作方式，提升医疗服务的整体质量与可及性。

Method: 通过工程研究中心（如CISST ERC）开展基础科学与工程研究，构建专业基础设施，促进计算机集成外科系统与技术的发展和应用。

Result: 实现了更精准的手术操作、更一致的治疗结果、更好的临床疗效、更高的患者安全性、更低的医疗成本、更快的患者康复，并为医疗提供者降低责任风险，同时开创了治疗健康问题的新方法。

Conclusion: 医疗机器人技术的进步将深刻影响整个医疗体系，得益于CISST ERC及其后续机构的工作，未来医疗将更加高效、安全和普惠，实现真正的‘治愈之触’。

Abstract: In the last ten years, medical robotics has moved from the margins to the mainstream. Since the Engineering Research Center for Computer-Integrated Surgical Systems and Technology was Launched in 1998 with National Science Foundation funding, medical robots have been promoted from handling routine tasks to performing highly sophisticated interventions and related assignments.
  The CISST ERC has played a significant role in this transformation. And thanks to NSF support, the ERC has built the professional infrastructure that will continue our mission: bringing data and technology together in clinical systems that will dramatically change how surgery and other procedures are done.
  The enhancements we envision touch virtually every aspect of the delivery of care: - More accurate procedures - More consistent, predictable results from one patient to the next - Improved clinical outcomes - Greater patient safety - Reduced liability for healthcare providers - Lower costs for everyone - patients, facilities, insurers, government - Easier, faster recovery for patients - Effective new ways to treat health problems - Healthier patients, and a healthier system
  The basic science and engineering the ERC is developing now will yield profound benefits for all concerned about health care - from government agencies to insurers, from clinicians to patients to the general public. All will experience the healing touch of medical robotics, thanks in no small part to the work of the CISST ERC and its successors.

</details>


### [212] [Semantic analysis of behavior in a DNA-functionalized molecular swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05277)
*Tom Bachard,Gong Yiming,Ibuki Kawamata,Akira Kakugo,Nathanael Aubert-Kato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将语义嵌入应用于学习分子群的行为范围，以提供更丰富的特征来优化此类系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高分子群系统模拟的可解释性和可靠性，需要更丰富的特征来描述其行为。

Method: 通过扩展微管模型并加入DNA功能化相互作用，利用语义嵌入从模拟结果中提取语义原子，并分解每一帧以分析外部控制值的影响。

Result: 提取的语义原子与预期行为一致，且每帧的分解准确反映了外部控制值的影响。

Conclusion: 该方法为模拟实验提供了可解释性线索，有助于设计和优化体外系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose applying semantic embedding to learn the range of behaviors exhibited by molecular swarms, thereby providing a richer set of features to optimize such systems. Specifically, we consider a standard molecular swarm where the individuals are cytoskeletal filaments (called microtubules) propelled by surface-adhered kinesin motors, with the addition of DNA functionalization for further control. We extend a microtubule model with that additional interaction and show that the extracted semantic atoms from simulation results match the expected behaviors. Moreover, the decomposition of each frame in the simulations accurately describes the expected impact of the external control values. Those results provide relevant leads towards the explainability of simulated experiments, making them more reliable for designing and optimizing in-vitro systems.

</details>


### [213] [Instantaneous Planning, Control and Safety for Navigation in Unknown Underwater Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05310)
*Veejay Karthik,Udit Ekansh,Tejal Bedmutha,Shivam Vishwakarma,Rohan Deshpande,Leena Vachhani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成的规划与控制框架，利用实时传感器数据实现自主水下航行器（AUV）在未知环境中的闭环轨迹生成，提升避障能力和狭窄空间内的机动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于水下环境中能见度低、信号传输弱和水流动态变化，AUV难以实现精确定位、可靠通信和有效避障，传统方法计算复杂且对传感噪声敏感。

Method: 设计基于预定义反馈控制器的运动规划方法，结合实时传感器数据，构建闭环控制系统，并通过ROS Gazebo仿真平台在RexRov AUV上进行验证，与PID跟踪方法对比性能，并量化航位推算中的定位误差。

Result: 所提方法降低了在线优化所需的计算复杂度，提高了复杂水下环境中的操作安全性，在仿真中展现出更优的避障性能和轨迹跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 该集成框架能够有效利用实时感知数据实现鲁棒的AUV闭环控制，适用于未知且复杂的水下环境，具有较强的实用性与发展潜力。

Abstract: Navigating autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) in unknown environments is significantly challenging due to poor visibility, weak signal transmission, and dynamic water currents. These factors pose challenges in accurate global localization, reliable communication, and obstacle avoidance. Local sensing provides critical real time environmental data to enable online decision making. However, the inherent noise in underwater sensor measurements introduces uncertainty, complicating planning and control. To address these challenges, we propose an integrated planning and control framework that leverages real time sensor data to dynamically induce closed loop AUV trajectories, ensuring robust obstacle avoidance and enhanced maneuverability in tight spaces.
  By planning motion based on pre designed feedback controllers, the approach reduces the computational complexity needed for carrying out online optimizations and enhances operational safety in complex underwater spaces. The proposed method is validated through ROS Gazebo simulations on the RexRov AUV, demonstrating its efficacy. Its performance is evaluated by comparison against PID based tracking methods, and quantifying localization errors in dead reckoning as the AUV transitions into the target communication range.

</details>


### [214] [ExpressMM: Expressive Mobile Manipulation Behaviors in Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05320)
*Souren Pashangpour,Haitong Wang,Matthew Lisondra,Goldie Nejat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ExpressMM的新框架，该框架结合了高层语言引导的规划器和低层视觉-语言-动作策略，用于在协作人机交互任务中生成具有表达性的机器人行为，并支持用户在执行过程中中断或重新定向指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表达性机器人行为研究大多未考虑在任务执行过程中用户可能中断、修改或重定向机器人行为的情况，因此需要一种能够实时适应人类指令变化的交互框架。

Method: 开发了ExpressMM框架，集成了基于视觉-语言模型的高层语言引导规划器与低层视觉-语言-动作策略，并在移动操作平台上实现，支持可中断的交互模式。通过在协作装配场景中的实际人机互动演示进行评估，并采用观众问卷方式进行评价。

Result: 实验结果显示，ExpressMM使观察者能清晰理解机器人的动作与意图，提升了交互的社会适切性与可理解性；参与者认为机器人在协作任务中有用、行为可预测且安全。

Conclusion: ExpressMM框架有效支持了动态、自然的人机协作，增强了机器人在人类中心环境中表达意图的能力，提升了用户对机器人安全性、可用性和可预测性的积极感知。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are increasingly deployed in human-centered environments to perform tasks. While completing such tasks, they should also be able to communicate their intent to the people around them using expressive robot behaviors. Prior work on expressive robot behaviors has used preprogrammed or learning-from-demonstration- based expressive motions and large language model generated high-level interactions. The majority of these existing approaches have not considered human-robot interactions (HRI) where users may interrupt, modify, or redirect a robot's actions during task execution. In this paper, we develop the novel ExpressMM framework that integrates a high-level language-guided planner based on a vision-language model for perception and conversational reasoning with a low-level vision-language-action policy to generate expressive robot behaviors during collaborative HRI tasks. Furthermore, ExpressMM supports interruptible interactions to accommodate updated or redirecting instructions by users. We demonstrate ExpressMM on a mobile manipulator assisting a human in a collaborative assembly scenario and conduct audience-based evaluation of live HRI demonstrations. Questionnaire results show that the ExpressMM-enabled expressive behaviors helped observers clearly interpret the robot's actions and intentions while supporting socially appropriate and understandable interactions. Participants also reported that the robot was useful for collaborative tasks and behaved in a predictable and safe manner during the demonstrations, fostering positive perceptions of the robot's usefulness, safety, and predictability during the collaborative tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Synergizing Efficiency and Reliability for Continuous Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05430)
*Chengkai Wu,Ruilin Wang,Yixin Zeng,Jiayuan Wang,Mingjie Zhang,Guiyong Zheng,Qun Niu,Juepeng Zheng,Jun Ma,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种兼顾效率与可靠性的连续移动操作统一框架，通过可靠性感知轨迹规划与相位依赖切换控制，在不确定环境中实现了高效且可靠的连续任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在复杂动态环境中难以像人类一样将长期规划与实时反馈结合以同时实现高效率和高可靠性，现有方法往往在追求效率时牺牲了稳定性与容错能力。

Method: 提出一种融合效率与可靠性的统一框架：包含可靠性感知的时空轨迹规划器，以及能在全局轨迹跟踪（效率）与任务误差补偿（可靠性）间相位依赖切换的控制器，并设计分层初始化机制以支持在线重规划。

Result: 在真实场景中验证了方法的有效性，相比最先进基线方法，在不同末端约束任务中任务成功率提升26.67%至81.67%，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功协调了长视野规划与实时响应之间的矛盾，在不确定环境下实现了高效、可靠、连续的移动操作，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans seamlessly fuse anticipatory planning with immediate feedback to perform successive mobile manipulation tasks without stopping, achieving both high efficiency and reliability. Replicating this fluid and reliable behavior in robots remains fundamentally challenging, not only due to conflicts between long-horizon planning and real-time reactivity, but also because excessively pursuing efficiency undermines reliability in uncertain environments: it impairs stable perception and the potential for compensation, while also increasing the risk of unintended contact. In this work, we present a unified framework that synergizes efficiency and reliability for continuous mobile manipulation. It features a reliability-aware trajectory planner that embeds essential elements for reliable execution into spatiotemporal optimization, generating efficient and reliability-promising global trajectories. It is coupled with a phase-dependent switching controller that seamlessly transitions between global trajectory tracking for efficiency and task-error compensation for reliability. We also investigate a hierarchical initialization that facilitates online replanning despite the complexity of long-horizon planning problems. Real-world evaluations demonstrate that our approach enables efficient and reliable completion of successive tasks under uncertainty (e.g., dynamic disturbances, perception and control errors). Moreover, the framework generalizes to tasks with diverse end-effector constraints. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, our method consistently achieves the highest efficiency while improving the task success rate by 26.67\%--81.67\%. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the contribution of each component. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [216] [CoEnv: Driving Embodied Multi-Agent Collaboration via Compositional Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05484)
*Li Kang,Yutao Fan,Rui Li,Heng Zhou,Yiran Qin,Zhemeng Zhang,Songtao Huang,Xiufeng Song,Zaibin Zhang,Bruno N. Y. Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Dongzhan Zhou,Wangmeng Zuo,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoEnv的多智能体具身系统框架，通过融合真实世界与仿真环境的“组合式环境”概念，实现多机器人在复杂操作任务中的高效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体具身系统在空间协调、时间推理和共享工作区感知方面面临挑战，现有方法难以兼顾安全规划与实际部署。受人类认知与动作分离协作方式启发，需构建统一决策空间以提升协作效率。

Method: CoEnv框架包含三个阶段：实景到仿真的场景重建、基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的动作合成（支持实时与迭代规划）、以及带碰撞检测的仿真到现实的安全迁移。该框架结合仿真进行策略探索，并确保现实世界的可靠执行。

Result: 在多臂操作基准任务中，CoEnv实现了高任务成功率和执行效率，验证了其在复杂协作任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: CoEnv为多智能体具身AI提供了一个新范式，通过虚实融合的架构解决了空间、时间和安全性协同的关键问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent embodied systems hold promise for complex collaborative manipulation, yet face critical challenges in spatial coordination, temporal reasoning, and shared workspace awareness. Inspired by human collaboration where cognitive planning occurs separately from physical execution, we introduce the concept of compositional environment -- a synergistic integration of real-world and simulation components that enables multiple robotic agents to perceive intentions and operate within a unified decision-making space. Building on this concept, we present CoEnv, a framework that leverages simulation for safe strategy exploration while ensuring reliable real-world deployment. CoEnv operates through three stages: real-to-sim scene reconstruction that digitizes physical workspaces, VLM-driven action synthesis supporting both real-time planning with high-level interfaces and iterative planning with code-based trajectory generation, and validated sim-to-real transfer with collision detection for safe deployment. Extensive experiments on challenging multi-arm manipulation benchmarks demonstrate CoEnv's effectiveness in achieving high task success rates and execution efficiency, establishing a new paradigm for multi-agent embodied AI.

</details>


### [217] [JailWAM: Jailbreaking World Action Models in Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05498)
*Hanqing Liu,Songping Wang,Jiahuan Long,Jiacheng Hou,Jialiang Sun,Chao Li,Yang Yang,Wei Peng,Xu Liu,Tingsong Jiang,Wen Yao,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了JailWAM，首个针对世界动作模型（WAM）的越狱攻击与评估框架，并构建了JailWAM-Bench基准，以系统评估WAM在越狱攻击下的安全对齐性能。实验表明该框架能高效揭示物理漏洞，并为构建安全可靠的机器人控制系统提供防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究极少关注WAM模型在安全性方面的关键缺口，尤其是其对越狱攻击的脆弱性。为了填补这一空白，本文旨在系统化评估并提升WAM在物理交互中的安全性。

Method: 提出JailWAM框架，包含三个核心组件：视觉-轨迹映射、风险判别器和双路径验证策略；同时定义三级安全分类框架，并构建JailWAM-Bench基准，在RoboTwin仿真环境中进行评估。

Result: 在RoboTwin环境中实验显示，JailWAM对最先进的LingBot-VA实现了84.2%的攻击成功率，有效暴露了WAM的物理漏洞，并可用于构建鲁棒的防御机制。

Conclusion: JailWAM为评估和增强WAM的安全性提供了有效工具，强调了安全对齐的重要性，为未来安全可靠的机器人控制系统的开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: The World Action Model (WAM) can jointly predict future world states and actions, exhibiting stronger physical manipulation capabilities compared with traditional models. Such powerful physical interaction ability is a double-edged sword: if safety is ignored, it will directly threaten personal safety, property security and environmental safety. However, existing research pays extremely limited attention to the critical security gap: the vulnerability of WAM to jailbreak attacks. To fill this gap, we define the Three-Level Safety Classification Framework to systematically quantify the safety of robotic arm motions. Furthermore, we propose JailWAM, the first dedicated jailbreak attack and evaluation framework for WAM, which consists of three core components: (1) Visual-Trajectory Mapping, which unifies heterogeneous action spaces into visual trajectory representations and enables cross-architectural unified evaluation; (2) Risk Discriminator, which serves as a high-recall screening tool that optimizes the efficiency-accuracy trade-off when identifying destructive behaviors in visual trajectories; (3) Dual-Path Verification Strategy, which first conducts rapid coarse screening via a single-image-based video-action generation module, and then performs efficient and comprehensive verification through full closed-loop physical simulation. In addition, we construct JailWAM-Bench, a benchmark for comprehensively evaluating the safety alignment performance of WAM under jailbreak attacks. Experiments in RoboTwin simulation environment demonstrate that the proposed framework efficiently exposes physical vulnerabilities, achieving an 84.2% attack success rate on the state-of-the-art LingBot-VA. Meanwhile, robust defense mechanisms can be constructed based on JailWAM, providing an effective technical solution for designing safe and reliable robot control systems.

</details>


### [218] [MARS-Dragonfly: Agile and Robust Flight Control of Modular Aerial Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05499)
*Rui Huang,Zhiqian Cai,Siyu Tang,Pengxuan Wei,Lidong Li,Xin Chen,Wenhan Cao,Zhenyu Zhang,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化空中机器人系统（MARS）的新型控制框架，通过机械设计与动力学建模结合，实现了稳定、敏捷的飞行与重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARS控制系统依赖简化的准静态模型和基于规则的分配方法，导致电机指令不连续且无界，随着无人机单元数量增加，姿态误差累积，引发严重振荡。

Method: 首先设计了一种紧凑的机械系统，实现被动对接、无检测锁定和磁辅助分离；其次引入力-力矩等效与多面体约束的虚拟四旋翼模型，显式建模可行的广义力集；最后构建两阶段预测分配管道，生成平滑可跟踪的电机指令。

Result: 在10多种构型的仿真和真实实验中验证了系统的稳定性与控制性能，首次实现了MARS在真实场景下的敏捷飞行与运输，最大俯仰角达40度，平均位置误差仅为0.0896米。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了MARS在动态重构过程中的控制难题，显著提升了系统的鲁棒性与敏捷性，为模块化无人机的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modular Aerial Robot Systems (MARS) comprise multiple drone units with reconfigurable connected formations, providing high adaptability to diverse mission scenarios, fault conditions, and payload capacities. However, existing control algorithms for MARS rely on simplified quasi-static models and rule-based allocation, which generate discontinuous and unbounded motor commands. This leads to attitude error accumulation as the number of drone units scales, ultimately causing severe oscillations during docking, separation, and waypoint tracking. To address these limitations, we first design a compact mechanical system that enables passive docking, detection-free passive locking, and magnetic-assisted separation using a single micro servo. Second, we introduce a force-torque-equivalent and polytope-constraint virtual quadrotor that explicitly models feasible wrench sets. Together, these abstractions capture the full MARS dynamics and enable existing quadrotor controllers to be applied across different configurations. We further optimize the yaw angle that maximizes control authority to enhance agility. Third, building on this abstraction, we design a two-stage predictive-allocation pipeline: a constrained predictive tracker computes virtual inputs while respecting force/torque bounds, and a dynamic allocator maps these inputs to individual modules with balanced objectives to produce smooth, trackable motor commands. Simulations across over 10 configurations and real-world experiments demonstrate stable docking, locking, and separation, as well as effective control performance. To our knowledge, this is the first real-world demonstration of MARS achieving agile flight and transport with 40 deg peak pitch while maintaining an average position error of 0.0896 m. The video is available at: https://youtu.be/yqjccrIpz5o

</details>


### [219] [Simulation-Driven Evolutionary Motion Parameterization for Contact-Rich Granular Scooping with a Soft Conical Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05531)
*Yongliang Wang,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Tomoya Takahashi,Masashi Hamaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理模拟的可变形软锥形机器人手模型，用于优化舀取任务的轨迹，无需手动调参，并在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人虽具有适应性强的优点，但其柔顺性和复杂变形行为导致控制复杂，限制了实际应用。因此需要一种能够准确建模并自动优化舀取轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出一种新的基于物理的仿真方法，精确建模软体工具从平面到自适应锥形结构的形态变化过程，并结合进化策略框架实现舀取轨迹的自动优化。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证了优化轨迹的有效性，结果表明该方法具有良好泛化能力，能成功完成一系列现有方法难以实现的挑战性舀取任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理仿真模型与进化优化框架相结合，显著提升了软体机器人在舀取任务中的自主性与实用性，为无需复杂传感与人工干预的智能操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tool-based scooping is vital in robot-assisted tasks, enabling interaction with objects of varying sizes, shapes, and material states. Recent studies have shown that flexible, reconfigurable soft robotic end-effectors can adapt their shape to maintain consistent contact with container surfaces during scooping, improving efficiency compared to rigid tools. These soft tools can adjust to varying container sizes and materials without requiring complex sensing or control. However, the inherent compliance and complex deformation behavior of soft robotics introduce significant control complexity that limits practical applications. To address this challenge, this paper presents the development of a physics-based simulation model of a deformable soft conical robotic hand that captures its passive reconfiguration dynamics and enables systematic trajectory optimization for scooping tasks. We propose a novel physics-based simulation approach that accurately models the soft tool's morphing behavior from flat sheets to adaptive conical structures, combined with an evolutionary strategy framework that automatically optimizes scooping trajectories without manual parameter tuning. We validate the optimized trajectories through both simulation and real-robot experiments. The results demonstrate strong generalization and successfully address a range of challenging tasks previously beyond the reach of existing approaches. Videos of our experiments are available online: https://sites.google.com/view/scoopsh

</details>


### [220] [Referring-Aware Visuomotor Policy Learning for Closed-Loop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05544)
*Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin,Xin Wen,Yixiong Li,Yuyu Sun,Yulan Guo,Liang Lin,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReV的闭环视觉运动策略框架，通过引入稀疏参考点实现对未预见情况的实时适应，在无需额外数据或微调的情况下提升了机器人操作中的鲁棒性和轨迹重规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉运动策略在面对分布外执行错误或需要动态重路由时缺乏鲁棒性，且通常依赖大量标注数据，难以适应动态变化的场景。

Method: 提出ReV框架，采用耦合扩散头结构：全局扩散头生成时空稀疏的动作锚点并确定参考点的时间位置，局部扩散头根据当前状态自适应插值相邻锚点，实现闭环实时轨迹调整；训练时仅通过对专家演示施加目标扰动，无需额外标注。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的复杂任务中，ReV在无额外数据或微调的情况下实现了更高的成功率，表现出优异的鲁棒性和动态适应能力。

Conclusion: ReV通过稀疏参考点和双扩散头机制，实现了高效、实时的轨迹重规划，为视觉运动策略在动态环境中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses a fundamental problem of visuomotor policy learning for robotic manipulation: how to enhance robustness in out-of-distribution execution errors or dynamically re-routing trajectories, where the model relies solely on the original expert demonstrations for training. We introduce the Referring-Aware Visuomotor Policy (ReV), a closed-loop framework that can adapt to unforeseen circumstances by instantly incorporating sparse referring points provided by a human or a high-level reasoning planner. Specifically, ReV leverages the coupled diffusion heads to preserve standard task execution patterns while seamlessly integrating sparse referring via a trajectory-steering strategy. Upon receiving a specific referring point, the global diffusion head firstly generates a sequence of globally consistent yet temporally sparse action anchors, while identifies the precise temporal position for the referring point within this sequence. Subsequently, the local diffusion head adaptively interpolates adjacent anchors based on the current temporal position for specific tasks. This closed-loop process repeats at every execution step, enabling real-time trajectory replanning in response to dynamic changes in the scene. In practice, rather than relying on elaborate annotations, ReV is trained only by applying targeted perturbations to expert demonstrations. Without any additional data or fine-tuning scheme, ReV achieve higher success rates across challenging simulated and real-world tasks.

</details>


### [221] [Uncovering Linguistic Fragility in Vision-Language-Action Models via Diversity-Aware Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05595)
*Baoshun Tong,Haoran He,Ling Pan,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DAERT的多样性感知具身红队框架，用于发现视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在语言变化下的漏洞，通过生成多样化且有效的对抗性指令，显著降低任务成功率，揭示其安全盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在实际部署中面临语言细微差异带来的鲁棒性不足问题，现有基于强化学习的红队测试方法易出现模式崩溃，难以全面揭示潜在风险。

Method: 设计了一种评估统一策略的方法，生成多样化的挑战性指令，并在物理模拟器中衡量执行失败情况以确保攻击有效性，从而实现对VLA模型的多样化压力测试。

Result: 在多个机器人基准上对π₀和OpenVLA等先进VLA模型进行实验，DAERT将平均任务成功率从93.33%降至5.85%，发现了更广泛且更有效的对抗指令。

Conclusion: DAERT为VLA模型提供了一种可扩展的、多样性感知的压力测试方案，能有效暴露其在语言变异下的安全盲区，有助于提升具身智能体部署前的安全性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable success in robotic manipulation. However, their robustness to linguistic nuances remains a critical, under-explored safety concern, posing a significant safety risk to real-world deployment. Red teaming, or identifying environmental scenarios that elicit catastrophic behaviors, is an important step in ensuring the safe deployment of embodied AI agents. Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising approach in automated red teaming that aims to uncover these vulnerabilities. However, standard RL-based adversaries often suffer from severe mode collapse due to their reward-maximizing nature, which tends to converge to a narrow set of trivial or repetitive failure patterns, failing to reveal the comprehensive landscape of meaningful risks. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel \textbf{D}iversity-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{E}mbodied \textbf{R}ed \textbf{T}eaming (\textbf{DAERT}) framework, to expose the vulnerabilities of VLAs against linguistic variations. Our design is based on evaluating a uniform policy, which is able to generate a diverse set of challenging instructions while ensuring its attack effectiveness, measured by execution failures in a physical simulator. We conduct extensive experiments across different robotic benchmarks against two state-of-the-art VLAs, including $π_0$ and OpenVLA. Our method consistently discovers a wider range of more effective adversarial instructions that reduce the average task success rate from 93.33\% to 5.85\%, demonstrating a scalable approach to stress-testing VLA agents and exposing critical safety blind spots before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [222] [Control Architecture and experimental validation of a Novel Surgical Robotic Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05610)
*Doina Pisla,Ionut Zima,Calin Vaida,Andrei Cailean,Marius Miclaus,Adrian Pisla,Andrei Caprariu,Vasile Bulbucan,Bogdan Gherman,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种4自由度的柔性腹腔镜器械控制架构，集成了远端弯曲、独立头部旋转、轴旋转和夹持器功能，直径保持在10毫米以内以兼容标准套管。系统基于树莓派5和Motoron控制器实现驱动与SpaceMouse遥操作，建立了分析性的剪叉连杆模型，并通过CAD数据和OptiTrack运动捕捉验证了 jaw opening 的精度。该器械与ATHENA并联机器人集成，并在模拟胰腺手术中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腹腔镜器械因运动范围有限而制约了微创手术（MIS）的发展，尽管MIS能减少患者创伤并缩短恢复时间，但仍需更灵活、精确的器械以提升手术操作性与安全性。

Method: 设计了一种4-DOF柔性腹腔镜器械，包含远端弯曲、独立远端头旋转、轴旋转和夹持功能，整体直径为10 mm；采用Raspberry Pi 5与Motoron控制器构建驱动与SpaceMouse遥操作系统；建立并参数化分析性剪叉连杆模型，用于预测夹爪开合角度。

Result: 所建模预测的夹爪开合角度与CAD测量值高度一致（平均绝对误差MAE为0.13°），并与OptiTrack运动捕捉结果吻合（MAE为1.43°）；成功将器械集成至ATHENA并联机器人，并在模拟胰腺手术中完成验证。

Conclusion: 该4-DOF柔性腹腔镜器械在保持小直径的同时实现了多自由度精确控制，具备临床应用潜力，且可与主从机器人系统结合，有望提升复杂微创手术的操作性能。

Abstract: Minimally invasive surgery (MIS) reduces patient trauma and shortens recovery time; however, conventional laparoscopic instruments remain constrained by limited range of movements. This work presents the control architecture of a 4-DOF flexible laparoscopic instrument integrating distal bending, independent distal head rotation, shaft rotation, and a gripper, while maintaining a 10 mm diameter compatible with standard trocars. The actuation unit and SpaceMouse teleoperation are implemented on Raspberry Pi 5 with Motoron controllers. An analytical scissor-linkage model is derived and parameterized. The predicted jaw opening corresponds to CAD measurements (MAE 0.13{\textdegree}) and OptiTrack motion capture (MAE 1.43{\textdegree}). Integration with the ATHENA parallel robot is validated through a simulated pancreatic surgery procedure.

</details>


### [223] [Grounding Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models Through Explicit Language-Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05614)
*Theodor Wulff,Federico Tavella,Rahul Singh Maharjan,Manith Adikari,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的训练框架，通过对比模型显式地将分层视觉-语言-动作（VLA）子任务描述与视觉观察和动作空间对齐，提升了机器人透明度和多模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层VLA模型在语言生成与动作执行之间缺乏显式的模态对齐，影响了机器人在人机协作中的透明性与可信度。

Method: 设计了一个基于对比学习的框架，用于评估生成语言与动作轨迹之间的对齐程度，并利用离线偏好学习优化语言-动作的联合表示。

Result: 在LanguageTable数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，性能接近全监督微调，同时显著减少了对昂贵标注数据的依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了VLA模型中语言与动作的对齐程度，为实现透明、可解释的人机协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving robot transparency is a critical step toward effective human-robot collaboration. To be transparent, a robot's natural language communication must be consistent with its actions and explicitly grounded in the task and environment. Existing hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can generate language (e.g., through chain-of-thought) and low-level actions. However, current work does not consider explicit alignment between these modalities during training. To address this crucial gap, we propose a novel training framework that explicitly grounds hierarchical VLA sub-task descriptions with respect to the visual observation and action space. Our framework uses a contrastive model to assess the alignment between generated language and corresponding action trajectories. This contrastive model enables direct ranking of different language-trajectory pairs based on their alignment, allowing us to refine the grounding of our hierarchical VLA through offline preference learning. We apply our framework to the LanguageTable dataset, a benchmark dataset of human language-annotated trajectories, and provide critical insights into multimodal grounding representations, all while establishing a strong baseline that achieves performance comparable to fully supervised fine-tuning and minimizing the need for costly data annotations.

</details>


### [224] [Leaderless Collective Motion in Affine Formation Control over the Complex Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05648)
*Jesus Bautista,Enric Morella,Lili Wang,Hector Garcia de Marina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过修改拉普拉斯矩阵权重来实现平面仿射编队集体机动的方法，利用复数表示简化2D仿射编队的分析与设计，仿真验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统领航-跟随策略限制了机器人集群的运动形式，难以实现复杂的集体运动。本文旨在提出一种无需领导者的编队控制方法，支持更广泛的集体运动模式。

Method: 通过修改用于实现静态编队的拉普拉斯矩阵的权重，将集体运动建模为参考构型的时间变化仿射变换，并使用复数表示相关变量以简化二维情况下的分析与设计。

Result: 得到了描述集体运动的解析解，能够显式设计定义运动的特征向量和特征值，并实现了包括平移、旋转、缩放和剪切及其线性组合在内的多种集体运动。

Conclusion: 所提方法在无领导者的情况下实现了灵活多样的仿射编队运动，具有良好的可扩展性和设计灵活性，适用于多机器人系统的协同控制。

Abstract: We propose a method for the collective maneuvering of affine formations in the plane by modifying the original weights of the Laplacian matrix used to achieve static formations of robot swarms. Specifically, the resulting collective motion is characterized as a time-varying affine transformation of a reference configuration, or shape. Unlike the traditional leader-follower strategy, our leaderless scheme allows agents to maintain distinct and possibly time-varying velocities, enabling a broader range of collective motions, including all the linear combinations of translations, rotations, scaling and shearing of a reference shape. Our analysis provides the analytic solution governing the resulting collective motion, explicitly designing the eigenvectors and eigenvalues that define this motion as a function of the modified weights in the new Laplacian matrix. To facilitate a more tractable analysis and design of affine formations in 2D, we propose the use of complex numbers to represent all relevant information. Simulations with up to 20 agents validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [225] [Rectified Schrödinger Bridge Matching for Few-Step Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05673)
*Wuyang Luan,Junhui Li,Weiguang Zhao,Wenjian Zhang,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Rectified Schrödinger Bridge Matching (RSBM) 框架，通过调节熵正则化参数统一生成式策略与最优传输，在仅3步积分内实现高成功率和低延迟的视觉导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型和Schrödinger Bridges的生成策略因高方差随机传输需要大量积分步骤，难以满足具身智能体实时控制的需求。

Method: 利用熵正则化参数ε连接最大熵传输（ε=1）与确定性最优传输（ε→0），提出共享速度场结构的RSBM框架；证明条件速度场在ε谱上形式不变，并设计线性降低ε以减少方差，支持粗粒度ODE积分。

Result: 在仅3次积分步长下，RSBM达到94%以上的余弦相似度和92%的成功率，显著优于传统方法所需的≥10步，无需蒸馏或多阶段训练。

Conclusion: RSBM通过统一视角桥接生成模型与最优传输，实现了高效、低延迟的视觉导航策略，推动了高保真生成策略在具身AI中的实际应用。

Abstract: Visual navigation is a core challenge in Embodied AI, requiring autonomous agents to translate high-dimensional sensory observations into continuous, long-horizon action trajectories. While generative policies based on diffusion models and Schrödinger Bridges (SB) effectively capture multimodal action distributions, they require dozens of integration steps due to high-variance stochastic transport, posing a critical barrier for real-time robotic control. We propose Rectified Schrödinger Bridge Matching (RSBM), a framework that exploits a shared velocity-field structure between standard Schrödinger Bridges ($\varepsilon=1$, maximum-entropy transport) and deterministic Optimal Transport ($\varepsilon\to 0$, as in Conditional Flow Matching), controlled by a single entropic regularization parameter $\varepsilon$. We prove two key results: (1) the conditional velocity field's functional form is invariant across the entire $\varepsilon$-spectrum (Velocity Structure Invariance), enabling a single network to serve all regularization strengths; and (2) reducing $\varepsilon$ linearly decreases the conditional velocity variance, enabling more stable coarse-step ODE integration. Anchored to a learned conditional prior that shortens transport distance, RSBM operates at an intermediate $\varepsilon$ that balances multimodal coverage and path straightness. Empirically, while standard bridges require $\geq 10$ steps to converge, RSBM achieves over 94% cosine similarity and 92% success rate in merely 3 integration steps -- without distillation or multi-stage training -- substantially narrowing the gap between high-fidelity generative policies and the low-latency demands of Embodied AI.

</details>


### [226] [Dynamic Control Allocation for Dual-Tilt UAV Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05677)
*Marcello Sorge,Federico Ciresola,Giulia Michieletto,Angelo Cenedese*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了六旋翼无人机在轨迹跟踪任务中的动态控制分配问题，提出了一种分层控制结构，并考虑执行器饱和及螺旋桨倾斜角度对控制性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高六旋翼无人机在复杂飞行任务中的控制灵活性和性能，尤其是在存在执行器饱和和系统冗余的情况下，需要有效的控制分配策略。

Method: 采用分层控制结构，包括高层控制器生成所需广义力，以及控制分配律利用系统冗余优化执行器状态；同时显式建模执行器饱和并分析其影响。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提出的控制分配策略的有效性，表明该方法能够有效处理执行器饱和问题，并通过优化螺旋桨倾斜角提升控制性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态控制分配方法能够在考虑执行器饱和的前提下，充分利用系统冗余，提升六旋翼无人机的轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper focuses on dynamic control allocation for a hexarotor UAV platform, considering a trajectory tracking task as as case study. It is assumed that the platform is dual-tilting, meaning that it is able to tilt each propeller independently during flight, along two orthogonal axis. We present a hierarchical control structure composed of a high-level controller generating the required wrench for the tracking task, and a control allocation law ensuring that the actuators produce such wrench. The allocator imposes desired first-order dynamics on the actuators set, and exploits system redundancy to optimize the actuators state with respect to a given objective function. Unlike other studies on the subject, we explicitly model actuator saturation and provide theoretical insights on its effect on control performances. We also investigate the role of propeller tilt angles, by imposing asymmetric shapes in the objective function. Numerical simulations are presented to validate the allocation strategy.

</details>


### [227] [GraspSense: Physically Grounded Grasp and Grip Planning for a Dexterous Robotic Hand via Language-Guided Perception and Force Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05697)
*Elizaveta Semenyakina,Ivan Snegirev,Mariya Lezina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Safina Gulyamova,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部可承受接触力图的抓取选择与力调节方法，将灵巧操作从纯几何问题转变为物理上合理的联合规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取规划方法通常假设物体表面力学性质均匀，忽略了在弱区域接触可能造成物体损伤的问题，需要一种考虑局部机械特性的抓取策略。

Method: 通过SAM3D重建目标物体3D几何模型，在Isaac Sim中导入并进行物理感知的几何分析，生成每个表面位置可承受的最大侧向接触力的力图；基于此力图对抓取候选进行筛选和重排序，并利用阻抗控制器根据接触点的局部可承受力调整各手指刚度。

Result: 在纸杯、塑料杯和玻璃杯上的实验表明，该方法能一致地选择结构更强的接触区域，并将握力保持在安全范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作将灵巧操作重新定义为结合抓取选择与握力执行的物理感知联合规划问题，为未来人形机器人系统提供了更安全可靠的抓取方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic manipulation requires more than geometrically valid grasps: it demands physically grounded contact strategies that account for the spatially non-uniform mechanical properties of the object. However, existing grasp planners typically treat the surface as structurally homogeneous, even though contact in a weak region can damage the object despite a geometrically perfect grasp. We present a pipeline for grasp selection and force regulation in a five-fingered robotic hand, based on a map of locally admissible contact loads. From an operator command, the system identifies the target object, reconstructs its 3D geometry using SAM3D, and imports the model into Isaac Sim. A physics-informed geometric analysis then computes a force map that encodes the maximum lateral contact force admissible at each surface location without deformation. Grasp candidates are filtered by geometric validity and task-goal consistency. When multiple candidates are comparable under classical metrics, they are re-ranked using a force-map-aware criterion that favors grasps with contacts in mechanically admissible regions. An impedance controller scales the stiffness of each finger according to the locally admissible force at the contact point, enabling safe and reliable grasp execution. Validation on paper, plastic, and glass cups shows that the proposed approach consistently selects structurally stronger contact regions and keeps grip forces within safe bounds. In this way, the work reframes dexterous manipulation from a purely geometric problem into a physically grounded joint planning problem of grasp selection and grip execution for future humanoid systems.

</details>


### [228] [Hazard Management in Robot-Assisted Mammography Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05749)
*Ioannis Stefanakos,Roisin Bradley,Radu Calinescu,Beverley Townsend,Tianyuan Wang,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SHARD和STPA的危险管理方法，用于确保在乳腺X光检查中辅助机器人MammoBot在临床环境中的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在早期开发阶段就确保与脆弱患者密切接触的医疗机器人的安全性，需要系统化的方法来识别和缓解潜在风险。

Method: 结合利益相关者引导的流程建模、软件危险分析与设计（SHARD）以及系统理论过程分析（STPA），共同定义机器人辅助工作流程，并识别技术、程序和人为交互中的安全隐患。

Result: 发现多数风险源于时序不匹配、操作过早或系统状态误读，而非组件故障；这些发现转化为更严格的安全需求，减少对人类操作时机和判断的依赖。

Conclusion: 该方法为临床辅助机器人系统提供了一种结构化、可追溯的安全驱动设计框架，具有广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic and embodied-AI systems have the potential to improve accessibility and quality of care in clinical settings, but their deployment in close physical contact with vulnerable patients introduces significant safety risks. This paper presents a hazard management methodology for MammoBot, an assistive robotic system designed to support patients during X-ray mammography. To ensure safety from early development stages, we combine stakeholder-guided process modelling with Software Hazard Analysis and Resolution in Design (SHARD) and System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). The robot-assisted workflow is defined collaboratively with clinicians, roboticists, and patient representatives to capture key human-robot interactions.
